#### [19. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Agent Orchestration & CLI Tools

* [SpaceApp (⭐12)](https://github.com/oll4com/spaceapp) ⭐ 12 | 🐛 97 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-16 - Self-hosted web workspace that runs Gemini CLI alongside OpenCode, Codex, Claude Code and other coding CLIs as side-by-side panes in persistent rooms, with MCP servers and managed browser panes. Install: `npx --yes run-spaceapp@latest install`. Source-available (BSL 1.1).

##### Commands & Extensions

* [MoltNode by Chord (⭐0)](https://github.com/tuantqse90/moltnode-mcp) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-16 - Gemini CLI extension that connects two hosted MCP servers and a `GEMINI.md` context file: MoltNode for JSON-RPC reads on 31 blockchains, and Chord for DEX swap quotes and unsigned swap transactions on Base, Monad, BNB Chain, Avalanche, Arbitrum, Robinhood Chain and Arc, plus token boards, candles and wallet PnL. No API key; the agent's own wallet signs every transaction. MIT. Install via `gemini extensions install https://github.com/tuantqse90/moltnode-mcp`.

##### Development Tools & Utilities

* [agy-powerline (⭐15)](https://github.com/wassim-azi/agy-powerline) ⭐ 15 | 🐛 6 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-16 - Fast, configurable statusline for Antigravity CLI (agy), the successor to Gemini CLI, with real-time 5h/7d Gemini quota meters, pace markers, context usage, and interactive TUI editor. Zero dependencies, MIT.
* [Check (⭐0)](https://github.com/golproductions/check-gemini) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-15 - Extension with a `BeforeTool` hook that checks each file write on your own machine before Gemini CLI makes it (and, on Linux, each shell command): files, folders, programs, git refs and packages it names must exist, and scripts must parse; what the machine proves wrong is blocked with the machine's message. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/golproductions/check-gemini`. Paid, 14-day free trial.

##### MCP Servers

* [DC Hub (⭐3)](https://github.com/azmartone67/dchub-mcp-server) ⭐ 3 | 🐛 14 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-17 - Live data for data-center site selection: a daily Data Center Power Index (DCPI) of where data centers can get grid power across 300+ markets, live grid feeds from the seven US ISOs, plus fiber, gas and electricity prices. Answers carry an as-of timestamp and a citation line. 94 tools, remote Streamable HTTP, free tier needs no key. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/azmartone67/dchub-mcp-server`.
* [AltoRank](https://altorank.co/docs/mcp/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Open-source SEO content software with a hosted MCP server: check whether search engines and AI crawlers can read a site, research keywords, read Google Search Console, and write sourced drafts into a person's review queue. No approve or publish tool. AGPL-3.0 and self-hostable ([source (⭐3)](https://github.com/AltoRank/altorank) ⭐ 3 | 🐛 13 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-16). Remote Streamable HTTP with OAuth sign-in. Add with `gemini mcp add --transport http altorank https://app.altorank.co/api/mcp`.
* [Google Hotels MCP (⭐1)](https://github.com/huynhtanan/google-hotels-mcp?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-16 - Search Google Hotels and Google Vacation Rentals from Gemini CLI: per-OTA nightly rates (Booking.com, Expedia, Agoda, Vrbo, Airbnb, Hotels.com and 10+ other providers), 3-month daily price calendars, amenities, nearby places and review topics. Local stdio server (3 tools) wrapping Apify Google Hotels actors; `uvx --from "git+https://github.com/huynhtanan/google-hotels-mcp.git@v0.1.2" google-hotels-mcp`.
* [Pethost](https://pethost.dev?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Hosting for Docker Compose projects with a remote MCP server (OAuth): Gemini CLI deploys a project, reads its logs and HTTP traffic, and rolls it back. Add it with `gemini mcp add --transport http pethost https://mcp.pethost.dev/mcp`.

#### [15. Static Analysis](/content/analysis-tools-dev/static-analysis/README.md)

##### Other / [Other](#other-1)

* [compose-lint](https://tmatens.github.io/compose-lint/) — Security linter for Docker Compose files. Checks compose.yaml against 27 rules taken from the OWASP Docker Security Cheat Sheet and the CIS Docker Benchmark: mounted Docker socket, privileged mode, missing no-new-privileges, extra capabilities, writable root filesystem, ports bound to all interfaces, credentials in environment variables, unpinned images. Each finding says how to fix it and which runtime error the fix can cause. JSON and SARIF output, a GitHub Action, and a dry-run auto-fix.

#### [19. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Commands & Extensions

* [brethof-brain (⭐1)](https://github.com/BrethofAI/brethof-brain-client) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-13 - Gemini CLI extension for the hosted brethof-brain memory: with every prompt, the records that bear on it arrive before Gemini answers; a brief at session start; every turn archived; the same memory is shared with Claude Code, Codex and other agents. Needs a brethof-brain account (free plan). Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/BrethofAI/brethof-brain-client`.
* [PostEverywhere (⭐0)](https://github.com/posteverywhere/gemini-cli-extension) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-10-14 - Social media scheduler for Gemini CLI. Write, schedule and publish posts to Instagram, TikTok, YouTube, LinkedIn, X, Threads, Pinterest and Bluesky, and plan a content calendar. Install with `gemini extensions install https://github.com/posteverywhere/gemini-cli-extension` ([posteverywhere.ai](https://posteverywhere.ai)).

##### Development Tools & Utilities

* [session-handoff-kit (⭐0)](https://github.com/alapha888/session-handoff-kit) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-10-16 - Agent Skill (open SKILL.md standard; installs into Gemini CLI with `npx skills add alapha888/session-handoff-kit --agent gemini-cli`) that ends a session with a structured handoff — goal state, decisions, files touched, open loops, next action — so the next session starts from facts instead of an over-full context. Also includes a context-hygiene checklist and a fill-in handoff template. MIT.

##### MCP Servers

* [Aident Loadout](https://aident.ai?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Hosted MCP server that gives Gemini CLI and other MCP clients one reusable setup for apps like GitHub, Slack, Gmail, Linear and Notion, plus 400+ expert-built Skills and built-in services (search, scraping, media generation) billed from one balance. Accounts are connected once in the browser and kept in Aident Vault, and every action is logged in Audit. Remote Streamable HTTP with OAuth sign-in; free pay-as-you-go start. Add with: `gemini mcp add --transport http aident https://loadout.aident.ai/mcp`.
* [CurlShot](https://curlshot.com/docs/guides/ai-agents?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Screenshot API with a hosted MCP server: `take_screenshot` renders any web page in a real browser and returns the image (PNG, JPEG, WebP or PDF; full page, device presets, ad and cookie-banner blocking), so Gemini CLI can see a page, and `list_devices` lists the presets. Remote Streamable HTTP with a CurlShot API key; the server is closed source. Free plan of 100 screenshots a month, no card. Add with: `gemini mcp add --transport http curlshot https://curlshot.com/api/mcp --header "Authorization: Bearer $CURLSHOT_API_KEY"`.

##### Documentation & Examples

* [Gemini CLI limits & pricing (QuotaBurn)](https://quotaburn.com/gemini-cli-limits/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Source-linked guide to Gemini CLI daily request quotas, the free-tier change, and per-token API cost.
